Rule 3345:
If grade value ?d AND type LevelOfEvidence, then type SourceGradeRecord.

Formal Rule:
Head: ?a  type  SourceGradeRecord
Body: ?a  hasGradeValue  ?d  ?d  type  LevelOfEvidence
